[ASSISTANT]
The testbench confirms the checking protocol. Now I'll write my optimized restructure: bit-slice opcode decode, per-lane 8-bit adder with explicit carry chaining, a single shared per-lane barrel shifter (left shifts via bit reversal), flattened clip/min-max path, and the verbatim serial divider (cycle-critical). Key insight I derived: the upstream CLIP's `adder_result_expanded[36]` equals `(a[31]&b[31]) | ((a[31]^b[31]) & ~carry_out)` of the lane-chained adder, so I can drop the 37-bit adder.
  -> create_file(content="// ============================================================================\n// Optimized execution-stage ALU of the CV32E40P (RI5CY) RISC-V core.\n//\n// I/O behaviour is cycle-identical to the upstream implementation\n// (starting_point.sv).  Restructured for area/delay:\n//   * compact bit-slice opcode decode (no 7-bit equality comparators)\n//   * per-lane 8-bit adder with explicit carry chaining\n//   * one shared per-lane barrel shifter (left shifts / rotations via the\n//     bit-reversal trick of the original)\n//   * flat result selection\n//   * the serial divider (cv32e40p_alu_div) is kept unchanged: it defines the\n//     cycle-accurate div/rem handshake.\n// ============================================================================\n\nmodule cv32e40p_alu (\n    input  logic               clk,\n    input  logic               rst_n,\n    input  logic               enable_i,\n    input  logic [ 6:0]        operator_i,\n    input  logic [31:0]        operand_a_i,\n    input  logic [31:0]        operand_b_i,\n    input  logic [31:0]        operand_c_i,\n\n    input  logic [1:0]         vector_mode_i,\n    input  logic [4:0]         bmask_a_i,\n    input  logic [4:0]         bmask_b_i,\n    input  logic [1:0]         imm_vec_ext_i,\n\n    input  logic               is_clpx_i,\n    input  logic               is_subrot_i,\n    input  logic [1:0]         clpx_shift_i,\n\n    output logic [31:0]        result_o,\n    output logic               comparison_result_o,\n\n    output logic               ready_o,\n    input  logic               ex_ready_i\n);\n\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8  = 2'b11;\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Opcode decode.  Only the 57 documented encodings are ever driven, so the\n  // families can be decoded from short bit-slices of operator_i.\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic op_addfam;   // ADD/SUB/ADDU/SUBU/ADDR/SUBR/ADDUR/SUBUR   0x18..0x1f\n  logic op_mmblk;    // MIN/MINU/MAX/MAXU/ABS/AND/CLIP/CLIPU      0x10..0x17\n  logic op_shfblk;   // SRA/SRL/ROR/SLL                           0x24..0x27\n  logic op_bmxblk;   // BEXT/BEXTU/BINS/BCLR/BSET/INS/OR/XOR      0x28..0x2f\n  logic op_div;      // DIVU/DIV/REMU/REM                         0x30..0x33\n  logic op_bitcnt;   // CNT/CLB/FF1/FL1                           0x34..0x37\n  logic op_packblk;  // PCKLO/PCKHI/SHUF/SHUF2                    0x38..0x3b\n  logic op_ext;      // EXTS/EXT                                  0x3e/0x3f\n  logic op_brev;     // BREV                                      0x49\n  logic op_cmpblk;   // the 14 comparison opcodes                 0x00..0x0d\n\n  assign op_addfam  = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0011);\n  assign op_mmblk   = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0010);\n  assign op_shfblk  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);\n  assign op_bmxblk  = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0101);\n  assign op_div     = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);\n  assign op_bitcnt  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);\n  assign op_packblk = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01110);\n  assign op_ext     = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01111) & operator_i[1];\n  assign op_brev    = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b1001) & ~operator_i[2] & ~operator_i[1];\n  assign op_cmpblk  = ~operator_i[6] & ~operator_i[5] & ~operator_i[4];\n\n  logic op_sub, op_round, op_addu;\n  logic op_and, op_or, op_xor;\n  logic op_bext, op_bextu, op_bins, op_bclr, op_bset, op_ins;\n  logic op_sra, op_srl, op_ror, op_sll;\n  logic op_cnt, op_clb, op_ff1, op_fl1;\n  logic op_minmax, op_abs, op_clip, op_clipu, op_min;\n  logic op_pcklo, op_pckhi, op_shuf, op_shuf2;\n  logic op_exts;\n  logic op_vcmp, op_scmp;\n\n  assign op_sub    = op_addfam &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_round  = op_addfam &  operator_i[2];\n  assign op_addu   = op_addfam &  operator_i[1];  // ADDU/SUBU/ADDUR/SUBUR\n  assign op_and    = op_mmblk   & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b101);\n  assign op_minmax = op_mmblk   & ~operator_i[2] & ~operator_i[1];  // MIN..MAXU\n  assign op_abs    = op_mmblk   &  operator_i[2] & ~operator_i[1];  // ABS\n  assign op_clip   = op_mmblk   &  operator_i[2] &  operator_i[1];  // CLIP/CLIPU\n  assign op_clipu  = op_clip   &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_min    = op_minmax & ~operator_i[0];\n  assign op_sra    = op_shfblk  & ~operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[0];\n  assign op_srl    = op_shfblk  & ~operator_i[1] &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_ror    = op_shfblk  &  operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[0];\n  assign op_sll    = op_shfblk  &  operator_i[1] &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_bext   = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b000);\n  assign op_bextu  = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b001);\n  assign op_bins   = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b010);\n  assign op_bclr   = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b011);\n  assign op_bset   = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b100);\n  assign op_ins    = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b101);\n  assign op_or     = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b110);\n  assign op_xor    = op_bmxblk  & (operator_i[2:0] == 3'b111);\n  assign op_cnt    = op_bitcnt  & ~operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[0];\n  assign op_clb    = op_bitcnt  & ~operator_i[1] &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_ff1    = op_bitcnt  &  operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[0];\n  assign op_fl1    = op_bitcnt  &  operator_i[1] &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_pcklo  = op_packblk & ~operator_i[1];\n  assign op_pckhi  = op_packblk & ~operator_i[1] &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_shuf   = op_packblk &  operator_i[1];\n  assign op_shuf2  = op_packblk &  operator_i[1] &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_exts   = op_ext     &  operator_i[0];\n  assign op_vcmp   = op_cmpblk  & ~operator_i[3];\n  assign op_scmp   = op_cmpblk  &  operator_i[3];\n\n  logic vm16, vm8;\n  assign vm16 =  vector_mode_i[1] & ~vector_mode_i[0];\n  assign vm8  =  vector_mode_i[1] &  vector_mode_i[0];\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Bit mask (bit-manipulation group and the rounding adder family)\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [31:0] bmask, bmask_inv;\n  assign bmask     = (~{32'hFFFFFFFE << bmask_a_i}) << bmask_b_i;\n  assign bmask_inv = ~bmask;\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Per-lane adder.  4 independent 8-bit lanes; carries are chained when the\n  // lanes are not partitioned.  carry-in of every lane is 1 for the\n  // subtract/abs/clip encodings (that is how the original partitions it).\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic        adder_neg;     // subtract: b inverted\n  logic        adder_rndin;   // every lane carry-in forced to 1\n  logic [31:0] adder_a, adder_b;\n  logic [8:0]  adder_s0, adder_s1, adder_s2, adder_s3;\n  logic [31:0] adder_result;\n\n  assign adder_neg   = op_sub | is_subrot_i;\n  assign adder_rndin = adder_neg | op_abs | op_clip;\n\n  assign adder_a = op_abs      ? ~operand_a_i\n                 : is_subrot_i ? {operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]}\n                               : operand_a_i;\n  assign adder_b = adder_neg   ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]}\n                                              : ~operand_b_i)\n                               : operand_b_i;\n\n  assign adder_s0 = {1'b0, adder_a[ 7: 0]} + {1'b0, adder_b[ 7: 0]} + 9'd1;\n  assign adder_s1 = {1'b0, adder_a[15: 8]} + {1'b0, adder_b[15: 8]}\n                    + (adder_rndin | adder_s0[8]);\n  assign adder_s2 = {1'b0, adder_a[23:16]} + {1'b0, adder_b[23:16]}\n                    + (adder_rndin | adder_s1[8]);\n  assign adder_s3 = {1'b0, adder_a[31:24]} + {1'b0, adder_b[31:24]}\n                    + (adder_rndin | adder_s2[8]);\n\n  assign adder_result = {adder_s3[7:0], adder_s2[7:0], adder_s1[7:0], adder_s0[7:0]};\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Normalization stage (adder family only): optional rounding constant,\n  // then a per-lane right shift.\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [31:0] adder_round_result;\n  logic [31:0] shift_amt_norm;\n  logic [31:0] sh_amt, sh_op;\n  logic        sh_left, sh_arith;\n  logic [31:0] shift_amt_left;\n  logic [31:0] shift_amt;\n  logic [31:0] shift_right_result, shift_left_result, shift_result;\n\n  assign adder_round_result = op_round ? (adder_result + {1'b0, bmask[31:1]})\n                                       : adder_result;\n\n  assign shift_amt_norm = is_clpx_i ? {4{2'b00, clpx_shift_i}}\n                                    : {4{3'b000, bmask_b_i}};\n\n  // amounts for the right shifter; div/rem use the leading-bit count of a\n  logic [5:0]  div_shift;\n  logic        div_signed, div_op_a_signed;\n  logic        div_valid;\n  logic [31:0] operand_a_rev, operand_a_neg_rev;\n  logic [5:0]  cnt_result;\n  logic [4:0]  ff1_result;\n  logic        ff_no_one;\n  logic [31:0] ff_input;\n\n  assign shift_amt = div_valid ? {26'b0, div_shift} : operand_b_i;\n\n  // by reversing the bits of the input, the order of the shift amounts is\n  // reversed as well\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shift_amt_left[15:0]  = shift_amt[31:16];\n        shift_amt_left[31:16] = shift_amt[15:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shift_amt_left[ 7: 0] = shift_amt[31:24];\n        shift_amt_left[15: 8] = shift_amt[23:16];\n        shift_amt_left[23:16] = shift_amt[15:8];\n        shift_amt_left[31:24] = shift_amt[ 7:0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shift_amt_left[31:0] = shift_amt[31:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign sh_left  = op_sll | op_bins | op_brev | op_div;\n  assign sh_arith = op_sra | op_bext | (op_addfam & ~operator_i[1]);\n\n  assign sh_op  = sh_left ? operand_a_rev\n                          : (op_addfam ? adder_round_result : operand_a_i);\n  assign sh_amt = op_addfam ? shift_amt_norm\n                            : (sh_left ? shift_amt_left : shift_amt);\n\n  // per-lane right shifter\n  logic [63:0] sh_op_32;\n  assign sh_op_32 = op_ror ? {sh_op, sh_op}\n                           : $signed({{32{sh_arith & sh_op[31]}}, sh_op});\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shift_right_result[31:16] = $signed({sh_arith & sh_op[31], sh_op[31:16]}) >>> sh_amt[19:16];\n        shift_right_result[15:0]  = $signed({sh_arith & sh_op[15], sh_op[15:0]})  >>> sh_amt[ 3:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shift_right_result[31:24] = $signed({sh_arith & sh_op[31], sh_op[31:24]}) >>> sh_amt[26:24];\n        shift_right_result[23:16] = $signed({sh_arith & sh_op[23], sh_op[23:16]}) >>> sh_amt[18:16];\n        shift_right_result[15: 8] = $signed({sh_arith & sh_op[15], sh_op[15: 8]}) >>> sh_amt[10: 8];\n        shift_right_result[ 7: 0] = $signed({sh_arith & sh_op[ 7], sh_op[ 7: 0]}) >>> sh_amt[ 2: 0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shift_right_result = sh_op_32 >> sh_amt[4:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  genvar j;\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_shift_left_result\n      assign shift_left_result[j] = shift_right_result[31-j];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign shift_result = sh_left ? shift_left_result : shift_right_result;\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Vector comparisons\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed;\n  logic [3:0] is_equal_vec, is_greater_vec;\n  logic [3:0] is_equal, is_greater;\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  logic       is_equal_clip;\n  logic [31:0] operand_b_eq;\n\n  // signed comparison selected by the opcode (complement of bit 0 in the\n  // compare / min-max families, always for abs and clip)\n  logic cmp_signed_en;\n  assign cmp_signed_en = (op_cmpblk & ~operator_i[0]) |\n                         (op_mmblk & (operator_i[2] |\n                                      (~operator_i[2] & ~operator_i[1] & ~operator_i[0])));\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE8:  cmp_signed = {4{cmp_signed_en}};\n      VEC_MODE16: cmp_signed = {2{cmp_signed_en}, 2'b0} & 4'b1010;\n      default:    cmp_signed = {3'b000, cmp_signed_en} & 4'b1000;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : gen_is_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[i] = (operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i] == operand_b_i[8*i+7:8*i]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[i] = $signed(\n          {operand_a_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i]}\n        ) > $signed(\n          {operand_b_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]}\n        );\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal[3:0]  = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater[3:0] = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                      | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                       | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])))))}};\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n        is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n        is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n        is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        is_equal[3:0]   = is_equal_vec[3:0];\n        is_greater[3:0] = is_greater_vec[3:0];\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    cmp_result = is_equal;\n    case (operator_i[2:0])\n      3'b000: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? is_greater             // GTS/GTU\n                                                      : ~(is_greater | is_equal))  // LTS/LTU\n                                     : is_equal;\n      3'b001: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? is_greater             // GTU\n                                                      : ~(is_greater | is_equal))  // LTU\n                                     : is_equal;\n      3'b010: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? (is_greater | is_equal)    // GES\n                                                      : ~is_greater)               // LES\n                                     : is_equal;\n      3'b011: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? (is_greater | is_equal)    // GEU\n                                                      : ~is_greater)               // LEU\n                                     : is_equal;\n      3'b100: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? (is_greater | is_equal)    // GES\n                                                      : ~is_greater)               // LES\n                                     : is_equal;\n      3'b101: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? (is_greater | is_equal)    // GEU\n                                                      : ~is_greater)               // LEU\n                                     : is_equal;\n      3'b110: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? is_equal               // EQ\n                                                      : ~is_greater)               // SLETS/SLETU\n                                                      : is_equal;\n      3'b111: cmp_result = op_cmpblk ? (operator_i[3] ? ~is_equal              // NE\n                                                      : ~is_greater)               // SLETS\n                                                      : is_equal;\n      default: cmp_result = is_equal;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Min / max / abs / clip\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [31:0] minmax_b;\n  logic [3:0]  sel_minmax;\n  logic [31:0] result_minmax;\n  logic        clip_lo, clip_zero;\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n\n  assign minmax_b = op_abs ? adder_result : operand_b_i;\n  assign sel_minmax = is_greater ^ {4{(op_minmax | op_clip)}};\n\n  assign result_minmax[31:24] = sel_minmax[3] ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24];\n  assign result_minmax[23:16] = sel_minmax[2] ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16];\n  assign result_minmax[15: 8] = sel_minmax[1] ? operand_a_i[15: 8] : minmax_b[15: 8];\n  assign result_minmax[ 7: 0] = sel_minmax[0] ? operand_a_i[ 7: 0] : minmax_b[ 7: 0];\n\n  // second equality comparator for clip\n  assign operand_b_eq  = op_clipu ? 32'b0 : ~operand_b_i;\n  assign is_equal_clip = (operand_a_i == operand_b_eq);\n\n  // overflow flag of the scalar (a - b) subtraction, recovered from the lane\n  // carries: 1 iff a >=s b for the clip subtraction\n  assign clip_lo    = ((operand_a_i[31] & operand_b_i[31]) |\n                       ((operand_a_i[31] ^ operand_b_i[31]) & ~adder_s3[8])) | is_equal_clip;\n  assign clip_zero  = operand_a_i[31] | is_equal_clip;\n\n  assign clip_result = op_clipu ? (clip_zero  ? 32'b0           : result_minmax)\n                                : (clip_lo    ? ~operand_b_i   : result_minmax);\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Shuffle / pack / extract / insert\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [3:0][1:0] shuffle_byte_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_reg_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg1_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg0_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_through;\n  logic [31:0]     shuffle_r1, shuffle_r0;\n  logic [31:0]     shuffle_r1_in, shuffle_r0_in;\n  logic [31:0]     shuffle_result;\n  logic [31:0]     pack_result;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_reg_sel  = '0;\n    shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b01;\n    shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b10;\n    shuffle_through  = '1;\n\n    case (operator_i)\n      7'h3f, 7'h3e: begin  // EXT / EXTS\n        if (op_exts) shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b11;\n        if (vm8) begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:1] = 3'b111;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[0]   = 1'b0;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:2] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[1:0] = 2'b00;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h38: begin  // PCKLO\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vm8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b0011;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0001;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h39: begin  // PCKHI\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vm8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b1100;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0100;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h3b: begin  // SHUF2\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[26];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[18];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[10];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[2];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h2d: begin  // INS\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b00;\n            case (imm_vec_ext_i)\n              2'b00: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1110;\n              2'b01: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1101;\n              2'b10: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1011;\n              2'b11: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b0111;\n            endcase\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel   = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] =  imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] =  imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_byte_sel = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      7'h3f, 7'h3e: begin  // EXT / EXTS\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h38: begin  // PCKLO\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h39: begin  // PCKHI\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b10;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h3b, 7'h3a: begin  // SHUF2 / SHUF\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = operand_b_i[25:24];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = operand_b_i[17:16];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = operand_b_i[ 9: 8];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = operand_b_i[ 1: 0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b0};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {operand_b_i[ 0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {operand_b_i[ 0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h2d: begin  // INS\n        shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shuffle_r0_in = shuffle_reg0_sel[1] ? operand_a_i\n                        : (shuffle_reg0_sel[0] ? {2{operand_a_i[15:0]}} : {4{operand_a_i[7:0]}});\n\n  assign shuffle_r1_in = shuffle_reg1_sel[1] ? {\n                            {8{operand_a_i[31]}}, {8{operand_a_i[23]}},\n                            {8{operand_a_i[15]}}, {8{operand_a_i[7]}}\n                          }\n                        : (shuffle_reg1_sel[0] ? operand_c_i : operand_b_i);\n\n  assign shuffle_r0[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[ 7: 0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_r1[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[ 7: 0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_result[31:24] = shuffle_reg_sel[3] ? shuffle_r1[31:24] : shuffle_r0[31:24];\n  assign shuffle_result[23:16] = shuffle_reg_sel[2] ? shuffle_r1[23:16] : shuffle_r0[23:16];\n  assign shuffle_result[15: 8] = shuffle_reg_sel[1] ? shuffle_r1[15: 8] : shuffle_r0[15: 8];\n  assign shuffle_result[ 7: 0] = shuffle_reg_sel[0] ? shuffle_r1[ 7: 0] : shuffle_r0[ 7: 0];\n\n  assign pack_result[31:24] = shuffle_through[3] ? shuffle_result[31:24] : operand_c_i[31:24];\n  assign pack_result[23:16] = shuffle_through[2] ? shuffle_result[23:16] : operand_c_i[23:16];\n  assign pack_result[15: 8] = shuffle_through[1] ? shuffle_result[15: 8] : operand_c_i[15: 8];\n  assign pack_result[ 7: 0] = shuffle_through[0] ? shuffle_result[ 7: 0] : operand_c_i[ 7: 0];\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Bit counting (popcount, find-first-one)\n  // ==========================================================================\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (\n      .in_i    (operand_a_i),\n      .result_o(cnt_result)\n  );\n\n  // the find-first-one tree is shared with the divider normalization\n  logic        div_signed_op;\n  assign div_signed_op = operator_i[0];  // DIV/REM\n\n  always_comb begin\n    ff_input = '0;\n    if (op_bitcnt | op_div) begin\n      if (op_ff1) begin\n        ff_input = operand_a_i;\n      end else if (op_fl1 | (op_div & ~div_signed_op)) begin\n        ff_input = operand_a_rev;\n      end else begin\n        // CLB and the signed div/rem ops\n        ff_input = operand_a_i[31] ? operand_a_neg_rev : operand_a_rev;\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  cv32e40p_ff_one ff_one_i (\n      .in_i       (ff_input),\n      .first_one_o(ff1_result),\n      .no_ones_o  (ff_no_one)\n  );\n\n  logic [5:0] clb_result;\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n\n  assign clb_result = {1'b0, ff1_result - 5'd1};  // 5-bit wrap, as upstream\n\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      7'h36:   bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};              // FF1\n      7'h37:   bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, 5'd31 - ff1_result};      // FL1\n      7'h34:   bitop_result = cnt_result;                                          // CNT\n      7'h35:   bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0)         // CLB\n                                        : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Bit manipulation (insert / extract / clear / set / reverse)\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic        extract_sign;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_and, bextins_result, bclr_result, bset_result;\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev, radix_4_rev, radix_8_rev, reverse_result;\n\n  assign extract_sign  = op_bext & shift_result[bmask_a_i];\n  assign bextins_and   = op_bins ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n  assign bextins_result = (bmask & shift_result) | (bextins_and & bmask_inv);\n  assign bclr_result   = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n  assign bset_result   = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[j] = shift_result[31-j];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 16; j++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*j+1:2*j] = shift_result[31-j*2:31-j*2-1];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 10; j++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*j+2:3*j] = shift_result[31-j*3:31-j*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b00:   reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n      2'b01:   reverse_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   reverse_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Serial divider (dividend = operand_b_i, divisor = operand_a_i)\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n\n  assign div_signed      = operator_i[0];\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n  assign div_valid       = enable_i & op_div;\n  assign div_shift       = (ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result)\n                           + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI      (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI     (rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(cnt_result == 6'b0),\n      .OpBSign_SI  (div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI   (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO      (result_div),\n      .InVld_SI    (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI   (ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO   (div_ready)\n  );\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Result selection\n  // ==========================================================================\n  always_comb begin\n    result_o = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      // logic\n      7'h15:   result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;  // AND\n      7'h2e:   result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;  // OR\n      7'h2f:   result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;  // XOR\n\n      // shifts and the adder family (normalized through the shifter)\n      7'h18, 7'h19, 7'h1a, 7'h1b, 7'h1c, 7'h1d, 7'h1e, 7'h1f,\n      7'h24, 7'h25, 7'h26, 7'h27:\n               result_o = shift_result;\n\n      // bit manipulation\n      7'h28, 7'h29, 7'h2a:\n               result_o = bextins_result;               // BEXT/BEXTU/BINS\n      7'h2b:   result_o = bclr_result;                 // BCLR\n      7'h2c:   result_o = bset_result;                 // BSET\n      7'h49:   result_o = reverse_result;              // BREV\n\n      // shuffle / pack / extract / insert\n      7'h3a, 7'h3b, 7'h38, 7'h39, 7'h3e, 7'h3f, 7'h2d:\n               result_o = pack_result;\n\n      // min / max / abs / clip\n      7'h10, 7'h11, 7'h12, 7'h13:\n               result_o = result_minmax;\n      7'h14:   result_o = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                    : result_minmax;    // ABS\n      7'h16, 7'h17:\n               result_o = clip_result;\n\n      // vector comparisons: replicate the flag into every lane\n      7'h00, 7'h01, 7'h04, 7'h05, 7'h08, 7'h09, 7'h0a, 7'h0b, 7'h0c, 7'h0d: begin\n        result_o[31:24] = {8{cmp_result[3]}};\n        result_o[23:16] = {8{cmp_result[2]}};\n        result_o[15: 8] = {8{cmp_result[1]}};\n        result_o[ 7: 0] = {8{cmp_result[0]}};\n      end\n\n      // scalar set-less\n      7'h02, 7'h03, 7'h06, 7'h07:\n               result_o = {31'b0, cmp_result[3]};\n\n      // bit counting\n      7'h34, 7'h35, 7'h36, 7'h37:\n               result_o = {26'h0, bitop_result};\n\n      // division\n      7'h30, 7'h31, 7'h32, 7'h33:\n               result_o = result_div;\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// population count\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// find first one\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input  logic [LEN-1:0]          in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0]  first_one_o,\n    output logic                    no_ones_o\n);\n\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n\n  logic [LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0]           index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar k, l2;\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n\n    for (genvar level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l2 = 0; l2 < 2 ** level; l2++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l2]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l2] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                              index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] :\n                                              index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node\n          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// simple serial divider for signed integers (int32) - unchanged\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    // input IF\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    //\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,  // gate this to 0 in case of unsigned ops\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,   // 0: udiv, 2: urem, 1: div, 3: rem\n    // handshake\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    // output IF\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  }\n      State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  // datapath\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n\n  // attention: logical shift in case of negative operand B!\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  assign ABComp_S = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? AddTmp_D + AddMux_D : AddTmp_D - $signed(AddMux_D);\n\n  // counter\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  // FSM\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // regs\n  assign RemSel_SN  = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN  = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(\n      OpA_DI\n  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;\n\n  assign AReg_DN   = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;\n  assign BReg_DN   = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ?   logic        adder_op_b_negate;
  logic [31:0] adder_op_a, adder_op_b;
  logic [35:0] adder_in_a, adder_in_b;
  logic [31:0] adder_result;
  logic [36:0] adder_result_expanded;


  assign adder_op_b_negate = (operator_i == ALU_SUB) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||
                             (operator_i == ALU_SUBU) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR) || is_subrot_i;

  // prepare operand a
  assign adder_op_a = (operator_i == ALU_ABS) ? operand_a_neg : (is_subrot_i ? {
    operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]
  } : operand_a_i);

  // prepare operand b
  assign adder_op_b = adder_op_b_negate ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{
    operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]
  } : operand_b_neg) : operand_b_i;

  // prepare carry
  always_comb begin
    adder_in_a[0]     = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[8:1]   = adder_op_a[7:0];
    adder_in_a[9]     = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[17:10] = adder_op_a[15:8];
    adder_in_a[18]    = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[26:19] = adder_op_a[23:16];
    adder_in_a[27]    = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[35:28] = adder_op_a[31:24];

    adder_in_b[0]     = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[8:1]   = adder_op_b[7:0];
    adder_in_b[9]     = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[17:10] = adder_op_b[15:8];
    adder_in_b[18]    = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[26:19] = adder_op_b[23:16];
    adder_in_b[27]    = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[35:28] = adder_op_b[31:24];

    if (adder_op_b_negate || (operator_i == ALU_ABS || operator_i == ALU_CLIP)) begin
      // special case for subtractions and absolute number calculations
      adder_in_b[0] = 1'b1;

      case (vector_mode_i)
        VEC_MODE16: begin
          adder_in_b[18] = 1'b1;
        end

        VEC_MODE8: begin
          adder_in_b[9]  = 1'b1;
          adder_in_b[18] = 1'b1;
          adder_in_b[27] = 1'b1;
        end
      endcase

    end else begin
      // take care of partitioning the adder for the addition case
      case (vector_mode_i)
        VEC_MODE16: begin
          adder_in_a[18] = 1'b0;
        end

        VEC_MODE8: begin
          adder_in_a[9]  = 1'b0;
          adder_in_a[18] = 1'b0;
          adder_in_a[27] = 1'b0;
        end
      endcase
    end
  end

  // actual adder
  assign adder_result_expanded = $signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b);
  assign adder_result = {
    adder_result_expanded[35:28],
    adder_result_expanded[26:19],
    adder_result_expanded[17:10],
    adder_result_expanded[8:1]
  };


  // normalization stage
  logic [31:0] adder_round_value;
  logic [31:0] adder_round_result;

  assign adder_round_value  = ((operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||
                               (operator_i == ALU_ADDUR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR)) ?
                                {
    1'b0, bmask[31:1]
  } : '0;
  assign adder_round_result = adder_result + adder_round_value;

[ASSISTANT]
My transcription of the adder carry structure was wrong. Studying the upstream carefully: the 36-bit adder has 9-bit fields (carry bit at 9k, operand byte at 9k+1..9k+8, result byte extracted from bits 9k+1..9k+8). The static carry-in constants implement: lane 0 gets +1 only for subtract/abs/clip; inner lanes chain the previous carry; partitioned boundary lanes get a constant +1 when the negate branch is active, else 0 (which swallows the crossing carry). Also my compare decode was wrong (SLTS vs GES share [3],[2:0] — bit 4 disambiguates). Let me rewrite with the corrected logic.
  -> create_file(content="// ============================================================================\n// Optimized execution-stage ALU of the CV32E40P (RI5CY) RISC-V core.\n//\n// I/O behaviour is cycle-identical to the upstream implementation\n// (starting_point.sv). / extract / insert\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [3:0][1:0] shuffle_byte_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_reg_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg1_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg0_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_through;\n  logic [31:0]     shuffle_r1, shuffle_r0;\n  logic [31:0]     shuffle_r1_in, shuffle_r0_in;\n  logic [31:0]     shuffle_result;\n  logic [31:0]     pack_result;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_reg_sel  = '0;\n    shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b01;\n    shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b10;\n    shuffle_through  = '1;\n\n    case (operator_i)\n      7'h3f, 7'h3e: begin  // EXT / EXTS\n        if (op_exts) shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b11;\n        if (vm8) begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:1] = 3'b111;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[0]   = 1'b0;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:2] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[1:0] = 2'b00;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h38: begin  // PCKLO\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vm8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b0011;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0001;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h39: begin  // PCKHI\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vm8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b1100;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0100;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h3b: begin  // SHUF2\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[26];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[18];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[10];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[2];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h2d: begin  // INS\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b00;\n            case (imm_vec_ext_i)\n              2'b00: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1110;\n              2'b01: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1101;\n              2'b10: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1011;\n              2'b11: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b0111;\n            endcase\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel   = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] =  imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] =  imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_byte_sel = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      7'h3f, 7'h3e: begin  // EXT / EXTS\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h38: begin  // PCKLO\n        if (vm16) begin\n          shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b01;\n          shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b00;\n          shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n          shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h39: begin  // PCKHI\n        if (vm16) begin\n          shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n          shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b10;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h3b, 7'h3a: begin  // SHUF2 / SHUF\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = operand_b_i[25:24];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = operand_b_i[17:16];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = operand_b_i[ 9: 8];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = operand_b_i[ 1: 0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b0};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {operand_b_i[ 0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {operand_b_i[ 0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n\n      7'h2d: begin  // INS\n        shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shuffle_r0_in = shuffle_reg0_sel[1] ? operand_a_i\n                        : (shuffle_reg0_sel[0] ? {2{operand_a_i[15:0]}} : {4{operand_a_i[7:0]}});\n\n  assign shuffle_r1_in = shuffle_reg1_sel[1] ? {\n                            {8{operand_a_i[31]}}, {8{operand_a_i[23]}},\n                            {8{operand_a_i[15]}}, {8{operand_a_i[7]}}\n                          }\n                        : (shuffle_reg1_sel[0] ? operand_c_i : operand_b_i);\n\n  assign shuffle_r0[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[ 7: 0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_r1[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[ 7: 0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                               (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_result[31:24] = shuffle_reg_sel[3] ? shuffle_r1[31:24] : shuffle_r0[31:24];\n  assign shuffle_result[23:16] = shuffle_reg_sel[2] ? shuffle_r1[23:16] : shuffle_r0[23:16];\n  assign shuffle_result[15: 8] = shuffle_reg_sel[1] ? shuffle_r1[15: 8] : shuffle_r0[15: 8];\n  assign shuffle_result[ 7: 0] = shuffle_reg_sel[0] ? shuffle_r1[ 7: 0] : shuffle_r0[ 7: 0];\n\n  assign pack_result[31:24] = shuffle_through[3] ? shuffle_result[31:24] : operand_c_i[31:24];\n  assign pack_result[23:16] = shuffle_through[2] ? shuffle_result[23:16] : operand_c_i[23:16];\n  assign pack_result[15: 8] = shuffle_through[1] ? shuffle_result[15: 8] : operand_c_i[15: 8];\n  assign pack_result[ 7: 0] = shuffle_through[0] ? shuffle_result[ 7: 0] : operand_c_i[ 7: 0];\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Bit counting (popcount, find-first-one)\n  // ==========================================================================\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (\n      .in_i    (operand_a_i),\n      .result_o(cnt_result)\n  );\n\n  // the find-first-one tree is shared with the divider normalization\n  always_comb begin\n    ff_input = '0;\n    if (op_bitcnt | op_div) begin\n      if (op_ff1) begin\n        ff_input = operand_a_i;\n      end else if (op_fl1 | (op_div & ~operator_i[0])) begin\n        ff_input = operand_a_rev;\n      end else begin\n        // CLB and the signed div/rem ops\n        ff_input = operand_a_i[31] ? operand_a_neg_rev : operand_a_rev;\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  cv32e40p_ff_one ff_one_i (\n      .in_i       (ff_input),\n      .first_one_o(ff1_result),\n      .no_ones_o  (ff_no_one)\n  );\n\n  logic [5:0] clb_result;\n  logic [5:0] bitop_result;\n\n  assign clb_result = {1'b0, ff1_result - 5'd1};  // 5-bit wrap, as upstream\n\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      7'h36:   bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};              // FF1\n      7'h37:   bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, 5'd31 - ff1_result};      // FL1\n      7'h34:   bitop_result = cnt_result;                                          // CNT\n      7'h35:   bitop_result = ff_no_one ? (operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0)         // CLB\n                                        : clb_result;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Bit manipulation (insert / extract / clear / set / reverse)\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic        extract_sign;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_and, bextins_result, bclr_result, bset_result;\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev, radix_4_rev, radix_8_rev, reverse_result;\n\n  assign extract_sign   = op_bext & shift_result[bmask_a_i];\n  assign bextins_and    = op_bins ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n  assign bextins_result = (bmask & shift_result) | (bextins_and & bmask_inv);\n  assign bclr_result    = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n  assign bset_result    = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[j] = shift_result[31-j];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 16; j++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*j+1:2*j] = shift_result[31-j*2:31-j*2-1];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 10; j++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*j+2:3*j] = shift_result[31-j*3:31-j*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b00:   reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n      2'b01:   reverse_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10:   reverse_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Serial divider (dividend = operand_b_i, divisor = operand_a_i)\n  // ==========================================================================\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & operator_i[0];\n  assign div_valid       = enable_i & op_div;\n  assign div_shift       = (ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result)\n                           + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI      (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI     (rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(cnt_result == 6'b0),\n      .OpBSign_SI  (div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI   (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO      (result_div),\n      .InVld_SI    (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI   (ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO   (div_ready)\n  );\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Result selection\n  // ==========================================================================\n  always_comb begin\n    result_o = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      // logic\n      7'h15:   result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;  // AND\n      7'h2e:   result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;  // OR\n      7'h2f:   result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;  // XOR\n\n      // shifts and the adder family (normalized through the shifter)\n      7'h18, 7'h19, 7'h1a, 7'h1b, 7'h1c, 7'h1d, 7'h1e, 7'h1f,\n      7'h24, 7'h25, 7'h26, 7'h27:\n               result_o = shift_result;\n\n      // bit manipulation\n      7'h28, 7'h29, 7'h2a:\n               result_o = bextins_result;               // BEXT/BEXTU/BINS\n      7'h2b:   result_o = bclr_result;                 // BCLR\n      7'h2c:   result_o = bset_result;                 // BSET\n      7'h49:   result_o = reverse_result;              // BREV\n\n      // shuffle / pack / extract / insert\n      7'h3a, 7'h3b, 7'h38, 7'h39, 7'h3e, 7'h3f, 7'h2d:\n               result_o = pack_result;\n\n      // min / max / abs / clip\n      7'h10, 7'h11, 7'h12, 7'h13:\n               result_o = result_minmax;\n      7'h14:   result_o = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]}\n                                    : result_minmax;    // ABS\n      7'h16, 7'h17:\n               result_o = clip_result;\n\n      // vector comparisons: replicate the flag into every lane\n      7'h00, 7'h01, 7'h04, 7'h05, 7'h08, 7'h09, 7'h0a, 7'h0b, 7'h0c, 7'h0d: begin\n        result_o[31:24] = {8{cmp_result[3]}};\n        result_o[23:16] = {8{cmp_result[2]}};\n        result_o[15: 8] = {8{cmp_result[1]}};\n        result_o[ 7: 0] = {8{cmp_result[0]}};\n      end\n\n      // scalar set-less\n      7'h02, 7'h03, 7'h06, 7'h07:\n               result_o = {31'b0, cmp_result[3]};\n\n      // bit counting\n      7'h34, 7'h35, 7'h36, 7'h37:\n               result_o = {26'h0, bitop_result};\n\n      // division\n      7'h30, 7'h31, 7'h32, 7'h33:\n               result_o = result_div;\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// population count\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// find first one\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input  logic [LEN-1:0]          in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0]  first_one_o,\n    output logic                    no_ones_o\n);\n\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n\n  logic [LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0]           index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar k, l2;\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n\n    for (genvar level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l2 = 0; l2 < 2 ** level; l2++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l2]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l2] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                              index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] :\n                                              index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node\n          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// simple serial divider for signed integers (int32) - unchanged\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    // input IF\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    //\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,  // gate this to 0 in case of unsigned ops\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,   // 0: udiv, 2: urem, 1: div, 3: rem\n    // handshake\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    // output IF\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  }\n      State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  // datapath\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n\n  // attention: logical shift in case of negative operand B!\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ?                      ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]}
                                    : ~operand_b_i)
                     : operand_b_i;

  assign adder_s0 = {1'b0, adder_a[ 7: 0]} + {1'b0, adder_b[ 7: 0]} + {8'b0, nb};
  assign adder_s1 = {1'b0, adder_a[15: 8]} + {1'b0, adder_b[15: 8]}
                    + ((vm8 ? nb : adder_s0[8]) ? 9'd1 : 9'd0);
  assign adder_s2 = {1'b0, adder_a[23:16]} + {1'b0, adder_b[23:16]}
                    + (((vm16 | vm8) ? nb : adder_s1[8]) ? 9'd1 : 9'd0);
  assign adder_s3 = {1'b0, adder_a[31:24]} + {1'b0, adder_b[31:24]}
                    + ((vm8 ? nb : adder_s2[8]) ? 9'd1 : 9'd0);

  assign adder_result = {adder_s3[7:0], adder_s2[7:0], adder_s1[7:0], adder_s0[7:0]};

                                       : adder_result;

  assign clpx_shift_ex   = $unsigned(clpx_shift_i);
  assign shift_amt_norm  = is_clpx_i ? {clpx_shift_ex, clpx_shift_ex}
                                     : {4{3'b000, bmask_b_i}};

  genvar kr;
  generate
    for (kr = 0; kr < 32; kr++) begin : gen_a_rev
      assign operand_a_rev[kr]     = operand_a_i[31-kr];

  // reversed as well
  always_comb begin

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// find first one
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
module cv32e40p_ff_one #(
    parameter LEN = 32
) (
    input  logic [LEN-1:0]          in_i,
    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0]  first_one_o,
    output logic                    no_ones_o
);

  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);

  logic [LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0]           index_lut;
  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;
  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;

  generate
    genvar j;
    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut
      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);
    end
  endgenerate

  generate
    genvar k, l2;
    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;

    for (genvar level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree
      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level
        for (l2 = 0; l2 < 2 ** level; l2++) begin : gen_node
          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l2]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2+1];
          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l2] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] == 1'b1) ?
                                              index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2] :
                                              index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l2*2+1];
        end
      end
      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level
        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node
          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two
            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];
            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];
          end
          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one
            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];
            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];
          end
          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range
            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;
            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;
          end
        end
      end
    end
  endgenerate

  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];
  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];

endmodule

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// simple serial divider for signed integers (int32) - unchanged
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
module cv32e40p_alu_div #(
    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,
    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6
) (
    input  logic                   Clk_CI,
    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,
    // input IF
    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,
    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,
    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,
    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,
    //
    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,  // gate this to 0 in case of unsigned ops
    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,   // 0: udiv, 2: urem, 1: div, 3: rem
    // handshake
    input  logic                   InVld_SI,
    // output IF
    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,
    output logic                   OutVld_SO,
    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO
);

  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;

  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;
  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;
  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;

  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;
  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;

  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;
  logic CntZero_S;

  enum logic [1:0] {
    IDLE,
    DIVIDE,
    FINISH
  }
      State_SN, State_SP;

  // datapath
  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));

  // counter
  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;

  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);

  // FSM
  always_comb begin : p_fsm
    State_SN   = State_SP;
    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;
    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;
    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;
    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;
    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;

    case (State_SP)
      IDLE: begin
        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;
        if (InVld_SI) begin
          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;
          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;
          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;
          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;
          State_SN  = DIVIDE;
        end
      end

      DIVIDE: begin
        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;
        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;
        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;
        if (CntZero_S) begin
          State_SN = FINISH;
        end
      end

      FINISH: begin
        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;
        if (OutRdy_SI) begin
          State_SN = IDLE;
        end
      end

      default: ;
    endcase
  end

  // regs
  assign RemSel_SN  = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;
  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;
  assign ResInv_SN  = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(
      OpA_DI
  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;

  assign AReg_DN   = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;
  assign BReg_DN   = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;
  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {
    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]
  } : ResReg_DP;

  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs
    if (~Rst_RBI) begin
      State_SP   <= IDLE;
      AReg_DP    <= '0;
      BReg_DP    <= '0;
      ResReg_DP  <= '0;
      Cnt_DP     <= '0;
      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;
      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;
      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;
    end else begin
      State_SP   <= State_SN;
      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;
      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;
      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;
      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;
      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;
      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;
      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;
    end
  end

endmodule

  end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:2] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[1:0] = 2'b00;\n        end\n      end\n\n      7'h38: begin  // PCKLO\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n
